I’ll sit there looking at a board packet or some vendor email, and it takes me forever to decide things I usually handle pretty quick.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any change in your appetite lately?\n\nPatient: A little, yeah. I don’t eat much during the day… mostly coffee, maybe a few bites of something. But then in meetings I’m the opposite sometimes, tapping my pen or shifting around like I can’t sit still.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, have you been feeling tense or on edge a lot?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… not really tense all the time. But I don’t want to hurt myself. I don’t have a plan or anything.\n\nDoctor: How distressed have you been feeling because of these changes?\n\nPatient: I guess… not a lot. It bothers me some, but I’ve been getting by. I try not to dwell on it too much.\n\nDoctor: For the past two weeks, have you felt this low, cloudy way most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. It’s been about two weeks, maybe since my husband retired and started being home all day. That’s when this… low, cloudy feeling seemed to settle in.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been worrying a lot more than usual?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little, but I wouldn’t say a lot more than usual.
